# MMS — Development Progress & Session Resumption

> **HOW TO RESUME:** When context window is getting full (>70%), start a fresh conversation, paste this file, and say:
> *"I'm continuing MMS development. Read PROGRESS.md and resume from where we left off."*
> Claude will read the active plan and dispatch subagents task by task.

## Confirmed Tech Stack (DO NOT CHANGE)

| Layer | Choice |
|---|---|
| Framework | Next.js 15 (App Router) + TypeScript |
| UI | shadcn/ui + Tailwind CSS |
| Database | Supabase — **same existing project**, 120 tables, 400k rows |
| Auth | Supabase Auth (existing, RLS already configured) |
| Data fetching | TanStack Query v5 |
| Deployment | Vercel |

**Critical DB facts:**
- `profiles` links to auth via `auth_user_id` (NOT `id`)
- `divisions` has: `id`, `name`, `short_name`, `color`, `is_active`, `sort_order`
- Schema reference: `Old Schema/` folder (16 .md files + `schema (1).sql`)
- Index of all 120 tables: `Old Schema/00_index.md`

## Folder Structure

```
D:/MMS/
├── src/
│   ├── app/
│   │   ├── (auth)/login/         ← Login page
│   │   └── (dashboard)/          ← All protected pages (layout has TopNav)
│   │       ├── layout.tsx
│   │       ├── page.tsx          ← Dashboard
│   │       ├── master-data/      ← Phase 1 partial
│   │       ├── purchase/         ← Phase 1 full
│   │       └── sales/            ← Phase 1 full
│   ├── components/
│   │   ├── ui/                   ← shadcn/ui
│   │   ├── shared/               ← reusable tables/dialogs
│   │   ├── layout/               ← TopNav, NavDropdown, DivisionFilter, UserMenu
│   │   ├── master-data/
│   │   ├── purchase/
│   │   └── sales/
│   ├── hooks/                    ← one file per module (useDivisions, usePurchaseOrders, etc.)
│   ├── lib/supabase/             ← client.ts (browser) + server.ts (async cookies)
│   ├── lib/utils.ts              ← cn() helper
│   └── types/database.types.ts  ← generated from Supabase
├── supabase/migrations/          ← new schema changes only
├── middleware.ts                 ← session refresh + route protection
├── Old Schema/                   ← reference only, DO NOT modify
├── Ideas/                        ← UI specs, DO NOT modify
├── docs/superpowers/specs/       ← design docs
└── docs/superpowers/plans/       ← implementation plans
```

| 2026-08-27 | **Bulk Tools — whole-branch review + P2b runtime-bug fix pass (Task 2b.6)** (branch `feature/bulk-tools`; migration `20260827000200`; closes the gap flagged in the P2b row above + fixes the operator-hit P2b Select bug + 4 other review Minors) | ✅ No secrets — 1 SQL migration (BEFORE UPDATE trigger, no new tables/columns) + React/hook edits; no keys/tokens; no dynamic SQL from user input. | ✅ **Closes the RLS-bypass gap the Task 2b.5 row (above) flagged and explicitly deferred.** New `guard_tool_unit_division_write()` / `trg_guard_tool_unit_division_write` (BEFORE UPDATE on `tool_asset_units`, `SECURITY DEFINER`, `SET search_path`, mirrors `guard_tool_tracking_mode_switch`'s pattern) re-checks `_user_has_permission(_current_user_data_id(), 'inventory.catalog.manage')` server-side whenever `division_id` changes — fires on the RPC's own UPDATE too (redundant-but-consistent) and on the direct-Edit path, which could previously bypass `rpc_transfer_tool_unit`'s gate entirely. Other column edits (condition/status/assigned_to/serial/brand/expiry) stay ungated (checked via `IS NOT DISTINCT FROM` on `division_id` only). `tool_asset_units` RLS itself is unchanged (`USING(true)`/`WITH CHECK(true)` to `{authenticated}` — tightening it would affect the whole unit CRUD path, still out of scope, now moot for this specific column). **Rolled-back probe (2026-08-27, real staging users, `request.jwt.claim.sub` + `SET LOCAL role authenticated`):** manager (`inventory.catalog.manage` via `is_system_admin`) changing `division_id` → **allowed**; non-manager (`field_rp` role, confirmed lacking the permission) changing `division_id` → **blocked** (`not authorized to change the owning division of a tool unit`); non-manager changing only `condition` (not `division_id`) → **allowed**. Forced rollback via `RAISE EXCEPTION` at the end — zero residue. | ✅ No new API routes/RPCs; no signature change to `rpc_transfer_tool_unit`. | ✅ Unchanged from 2b.3/2b.5 — hooks still throw raw errors → toast; the new trigger raises a specific, actionable message. | ✅ **Fixed the operator-hit P2b bug** — `ToolUnitTransferDialog`'s destination `Select` used `value={toDivisionId \|\| undefined}`, which made the `value` prop `undefined` whenever nothing was picked and a real string once it was — Base UI's `useControlled` locks controlled/uncontrolled mode from the first render, so this flip is exactly the "changing an uncontrolled component to controlled" antipattern (confirmed by reading `@base-ui/react/select`'s `SelectRoot` source). Changed to `value={toDivisionId}` (always a defined string, `''` or a real id) — controlled for the dialog's whole lifetime; the trigger's own `<span>` already resolves `''` to the placeholder independently of Base UI's internal value-matching, so no visible change. Also fixed **dirty-on-open**: `isDirty` compared `toDivisionId` against `''`, so a pre-seeded single-option transfer opened already "dirty"; now compares against `seededToDivisionId` (the same value the seeding effect computes), so a pristine single-option open is not dirty and an actual pick still is. Division-name display across all 3 surfaces (`ToolCategoryRow`'s unit row, `ToolAssetEditDialog`'s unit edit dialog, `ToolUnitTransferDialog`'s current-division) now resolves against an **all-divisions** lookup (`useAllDivisions`, not just the active-only `useDivisions`) and shows **"Inactive division"** — the real name suffixed `(inactive)` when resolvable, a bare fallback string only if not — instead of misreporting a since-deactivated owning division as "Unassigned" (previously conflated: both rendered identically). Traced the edit dialog's seed (`unit?.division_id ?? …`) and save (`division_id: divisionId \|\| null`) paths end-to-end to confirm a stale/inactive id already round-trips correctly today — this was a **display-only** bug, not a data-loss one, and the fix touches only the trigger's `<span>` text, never the seeding/save logic. | ✅ Every touched Select/trigger keeps its existing fixed `h-10 w-full min-w-0` sizing — no layout change, display-text-only fixes. Also fixed 2 unrelated Minors surfaced by the same review: `ToolCategoryRow.tsx`'s bulk-vs-serialized row ternary (`{expanded && bulk ? bulkRows : serializedRows}`) parsed as `(expanded && bulk) ? bulkRows : serializedRows` — the serialized branch was technically "live" whenever collapsed, safe only because `toolItems` is `[]` while collapsed (`useInventoryItemsByCategory(expanded ? node.id : null, …)`); parenthesized to `{expanded && (bulk ? … : …)}` — explicit guard, zero behavior change. `useCategoryHasStockOrUnits.ts`: added `.limit(1)` to both `head:true` count queries (Supabase-budget-rule literal compliance — harmless, `count:'exact'` reflects the full per-chunk match regardless of the row-window limit) and chunked all 3 `.in()` lookups (`item_id`×2 sharing the same up-to-1000-id array, `brand_variant_id`×1) at `CHUNK=200` like `useCascadeAccessibleItems` — previously a large category's `ids`/`bvIds` array could exceed the PostgREST URL-length cap and throw, failing the client-side mode-toggle guard **open** (the server trigger `trg_guard_tool_tracking_mode_switch` still blocked the actual switch either way — UX-only bug, never a security one). | Migration applied to **staging only** (`mwvblpgbgxipvrevkeff`) + mirrored to `migrations-staging/`; live-verified before writing (zero pre-existing triggers on `tool_asset_units`, exactly 1 permissive RLS policy `USING(true)/WITH CHECK(true)` to `{authenticated}`, both permission helpers' bodies fetched via `pg_get_functiondef`). Flow-registry [[Transfer Serialized Tool Unit]] entry updated — gap marked **CLOSED** with the probe results, Guards/Docs sections updated to reference the new trigger + migration. Full-branch `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npx eslint src` both clean (0 errors, exit 0). ⏸ **Operator browser smoke still pending** (unchanged scope from 2b.5 — this pass fixes whole-branch-review + runtime findings only, no new golden-path surface to test beyond confirming the transfer dialog no longer misbehaves). Not merged, not on new-prod. |

### 2026-05-16 Full Codebase Audit — Detail

**1. Secrets** ✅ CLEAN
- All API tokens (`WATI_API_TOKEN`, `DIBSY_SECRET_KEY`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`) loaded from `process.env` only. No hardcoded credentials found in source.
- `.gitignore` covers `.env*` and `*.pem`. `.env.local` is present on disk but gitignored (verify it has never been committed: `git log --all -- .env.local`).

**2. RLS** ✅ CLEAN
- Migration `20260420000002_fix_rls_all_tables.sql` enables RLS + a permissive `authenticated` policy on all 48+ tables.
- All new tables added since (employee_services, chat_messages, chat_conversations, team_activity_log, tool_assignments, etc.) each have RLS enabled in their own migration.
- **Design note:** Policies use `USING (true)` — all authenticated users can read/write all rows. Intentional for this internal ERP. Multi-tenant row isolation is a Phase 3 concern.

**3. Auth Gate** ⚠️ FIXED THIS SESSION
- Middleware correctly blocks unauthenticated access to all routes.
- **Bug found & fixed:** `ALLOWED_PREFIXES` in `middleware.ts` was defined but never used — a dead-code oversight meaning external webhook calls (Wati, 17track) were being redirected to `/login` instead of reaching their handlers. Fixed by replacing it with `WEBHOOK_PREFIXES` and applying the check (`src/middleware.ts`).
- Webhook routes validate their own payloads internally (Wati by sender phone match, 17track by shared secret).

**4. Error Handling** ⚠️ FIXED THIS SESSION
- Most routes have full try/catch and return proper status codes.
- **Bug found & fixed:** `POST /api/wati/send-message` was returning `{ ok: true }` even when the Wati API call silently failed. Fixed — response now includes `watiSent: boolean` and `watiError: string | null` so callers know if delivery actually happened (`src/app/api/wati/send-message/route.ts`).
- `POST /api/shipments/deregister-tracking` swallows the tracking error and returns 200 intentionally — deregistering is non-fatal (fire-and-forget after a shipment closes). Accepted.

✅ **Approval-tier Add fix + PO item-picker INP — SHIPPED TO PROD** 2026-08-15 (pushed `2227a832..982403fe` → Vercel rebuild). **(a) Add Tier duplicate-key fix** (`982403fe`): `duplicate key … approval_chain_tiers_chain_id_rank_key` on Add Tier — root cause was `nextRank = visibleCount+1` colliding with a **live** tier when ranks are non-contiguous (new-prod ranks were 1,3,4 — a gap; verified **0 soft-deleted rows**, nothing to clean). Now `nextRank = max(rank)+1` over ALL physical tiers; tier delete switched **soft→hard** (no FK references `po_approval_chain_tiers`, approvers are snapshotted at submission — the soft delete only left the row occupying `UNIQUE(chain_id,rank)` and blocking the next add); config table now sorts by **amount band, not rank** (approval is parallel → order is display-only). **(b) PO item-picker INP** (`f1e2bf74`): opening the Item dropdown mounted up to 100 cmdk rows synchronously (~208ms; each row = pre-paint useLayoutEffect + photo + 2 icons) → now renders **30 rows before typing**, full search on type. `tsc` clean; no DB change/migration. **Next design thread:** rip out item "Sharing" → explicit item→division assignment + per-division pools + transfer-only (operator decided); cross-category handling = Option B (per-division category overlay) under discussion.

✅ **Shipped to prod (push `142efbf5..af021673`, Vercel rebuild) — PO picker fixes + FIFO + thin band + Pass-1 inventory catalog.** **(1)** PO item-picker **membership fix** (`1ed3452b`): the cascade reused the consume/sell filter (`accessible = owned ∪ shared∩has_stock`), so on a zero-stock catalog every item was hidden → "No categories found", blocking PO creation. Added a `requireStock` mode; PO passes `divisionFilterRequiresStock=false` → an item shared to the line's division is purchasable regardless of stock (consume/sell keep the stock rule). **(2)** PO cascade **perf fix** (`af021673`): category + item selects fed the full accessible set into cmdk (renders every option, re-scores all per keystroke) → ~11s INP on item search. Now own-filter (`shouldFilter=false`) + cap 100 rows + "keep typing" hint. **(3)** FIFO intake 6-at-a-time + animation (`234009db`). **(4)** Thin PDF doc band 9→4mm (`043f3e17`). **Also loaded earlier today:** corrected inventory catalog (Pass-1) to new-prod — 449 cats / 950 items / 1044 variants. **VWh Phase-1 DB migrations (`000700`–`001000`) ride in the branch but are NOT applied to new-prod** (no UI references them yet — apply when the Projects UI ships). ✅ **Multi-Division PO — Phase 2 (receiving routing + money-out attribution) — SHIPPED TO PROD** on `deploy/warehouse-shipping` (commits `08bec1a8`/`fc6c12bb` receiving · `8ac1f5b5`/`c2336b6a` money-out; pushed `57014f76..c2336b6a` → Vercel prod rebuild; 3 migrations `000400`/`000500`/`000600` applied to new-prod + object-verified). **(a) Per-division receiving routing** (migration `20260824000400`): `create_and_approve_receival` accepts a sub-container in ANY of the PO's `division_ids` and enforces **per-line routing** (a line can only be received into its own division's bin; multi-division POs require an explicit sub-container). `PoReceiveTab`: division-aware sub-container filter, non-matching rows lock as "Other division" (excluded from submit), operator note. DB-probe-verified (Kitchen-line→Maintenance-bin blocked; Maintenance-line→Maintenance-bin RCV-00032 succeeded, rolled back). **(b) Stock-driven reports auto-attribute per division** (verified: `rpc_report_product_cost` groups by `warehouse_sub_containers.division_id`; revenue-cogs by consumption division; P&L inventory side by sub-container). **(c) Money-OUT per-division attribution (Option B, report-only — migration `20260824000500`)**: new `_po_division_weights(po_id)` (line-value shares, weights sum to 1, INVOKER + REVOKEd from anon/authenticated) is the single source of truth; `rpc_report_accounts_payable` expands each bill into one row per division (amount/paid pro-rata, **rounding residual → largest slice** so rows tie back to the bill total exactly; visibility + `p_division_ids` now key on the ALLOCATED division); `rpc_report_pnl` splits `v_fx` + cash-basis `cash_out` for purchase payments by the same weights (incoming/sales + accrual COGS unchanged); the FX drill-down `rpc_report_pnl_fx_detail` (migration `20260824000600`, +`division_id`/`division_name` cols + Division column in the dialog) splits the same way so it reconciles with the P&L `fx_net`. **The Cash report (`rpc_report_cash`) is deliberately NOT split** — a running-balance cash ledger must show whole payments (splitting one real outflow would corrupt the balance). **No bill/payment row is written differently** — reads-only expansion, grand totals preserved by construction. Frontend: payables page "Bills" stat now counts distinct bills (a split bill is one bill, N rows); FX dialog gains a Division column + composite row key. Rolled-back owner-JWT probes verified: 3-way split 100.00→33.34/33.33/33.33; real PO-2026-08-007 5000.00 bill→Maintenance 2659.19/Kitchen 2340.81 (ties to 5000.00); one-division filter→1 slice; P&L cash_out invariance; AP real-data total preserved (243,670.00 over 8 bills); FX drill-down ties to `fx_net` (−25,147.70). `tsc`+eslint clean. Per-division/split approval = out of scope. **All 3 migrations now on new-prod + verified (helper/ap_split/fx_div_cols/receiving_multidiv all present); frontend pushed → Vercel prod rebuild.** Recommended post-ship operator smoke: Reports → Accounts Payable on a multi-division bill (one row per division, subtotals, grand total = bill).

✅ **Multi-Division Purchase Order (Phase 1) — COMPLETE, browser-tested, SHIPPED TO PROD** on `deploy/warehouse-shipping` (commits `e4c61e98` db · `61aa37f4` frontend; pushed `4c5622ee..61aa37f4` → Vercel prod deploy). One PO can hold line items belonging to different divisions (same supplier, one amount-tier approval). **DB:** `po_line_items.division_id` + `purchase_orders.division_ids uuid[]` (backfilled) kept current by the `po_recompute_division_ids` trigger; PO RLS repointed to array-aware `is_any_division_visible(division_ids)` (CASE: array when set, header fallback for legacy → no regression); **`is_division_member`** added for per-line access checks (NOT the active-division-narrowed `is_division_visible`, which would wrongly block legit cross-division lines — caught during Task 8); `rpc_create_purchase_order` + `rpc_replace_po_lines` carry per-line division (fallback to header) + enforce membership (**fixes the edit-wipe**: editing no longer drops line divisions). **Frontend:** per-line **Division selector** in `PoLineItemsEditor` (re-scopes each line's item cascade via a new `CascadeInventorySelector.divisionId` prop); create-po "Default division for new lines" + per-line payload + validation; edit-po seeds + carries division; orders list **"Multi (N)"** badge + the client filter now checks `division_ids` (mixed PO no longer hides under a non-primary division); PO-detail + receiving per-line badges; types patched. - **Phase F — Damaged Stock On-hand actions** ✅ shipped. Send-for-Repair + Write-off available as per-row icon buttons on `/warehouse/damaged-stock` → On-hand. Send-for-Repair fires immediately (no approval); write-off routes through the `stock_adj` approval chain via `source_pile='damaged'` on `stock_adjustments`. 4 migrations + 4 new files. Return-from-repair for ad-hoc transfers already works via the Phase E-relaxed logic.
- **Phase G — Receival FX conversion** (pending). Right now `receival_items.unit_cost` is stored as the PO's raw currency amount, and downstream `fifo_cost_layers.unit_cost` / `inventory_damaged_stock.weighted_unit_cost` inherit that raw value while labeling it QAR. Any non-QAR PO produces stock rows whose displayed cost is wrong. Scope: attach the currency + FX rate at receival time (either as a new column on `fifo_cost_layers` or convert-to-QAR at insertion using the PO's booked rate). Touches every downstream cost display (Inventory Stock Overview, Stock Value, Damaged Stock weighted cost, FIFO layers table). Non-trivial — expect a full plan doc.

## 📝 Conventions

- **Number formatting (UI):** money + counts render as `##,###.##` (comma thousands, dot decimal). Use `formatCurrency(value, 'QAR')` for currency, `formatNumber(value)` for counts. Inline `.toLocaleString()` calls are pinned to `'en-QA'` so the format is deterministic regardless of browser locale.

- [2026-08-10] **Security P1 — purchase_orders financial-column lock + Save-as-Draft pull-back** — `supabase/migrations/20260819130000_guard_po_locked_columns.sql` (+ staging mirror), `src/hooks/usePurchaseOrders.ts`, `src/app/(dashboard)/purchase/edit-po/[id]/page.tsx`. Branch `feature/security-p1-po-column-lock` (off `deploy/warehouse-shipping`); STAGING-only + mirror; **not pushed**. Two changes, operator-confirmed, `tsc` clean: (1) **Column-lock guard** (commit `54c7dd9e`) — `guard_po_locked_columns()` BEFORE UPDATE trigger (SECURITY INVOKER, `SET search_path=public`) blocks a direct client write (`current_user in authenticated/anon`) that changes any financial/commitment column (supplier_id/name, currency, exchange_rate, initial_exchange_rate, subtotal, total_qar, discount_amount/label, payment_terms(+notes), payment_milestones, delivery_terms(+notes), expected_delivery, quote_deadline) **while the PO stays in a locked status** (approved/partially_received/received/completed/cancelled). The `NEW.status = OLD.status` gate lets the amend flow (which transitions out → pending_approval in the same UPDATE) pass; every `purchase_orders` writer is SECURITY DEFINER except the pdf-invalidate trigger (touches no locked column), so no legit flow breaks. Closes the "non-status column edit on a locked PO" gap from follow-up B. (2) **Save-as-Draft pull-back** (commit `0e5d0040`) — `useSavePoAsDraft` never changed `status`, so on an approved/pending PO it silently rewrote the amounts in place (which the new guard now blocks). It now sets `status='draft' + po_type='draft'` when the PO was approved/pending_approval, tears down the in-flight approval chain (`rpc_clear_po_approval_steps` p_only_pending + dismisses `po_approval_requested` notifications, mirroring `useRecallPOToDraft`), and logs "PO Pulled Back to Draft"; draft/RFQ POs unchanged. The amend confirm dialog now branches on save kind ("Pull the PO back to Draft" vs the unchanged Submit path). - [2026-08-07] **Credit-customer credit restoration on payment (future-plans HIGH)** — `supabase/migrations/20260815010000_fix_credit_restore_17arg_create_sale_order.sql` (+ staging mirror). Two `create_sale_order` overloads existed on staging: the 17-arg (`p_division_id DEFAULT NULL`) the app calls, and a dead 18-arg (`p_subtotal, p_notes` signature — no callers in `src/`). The 17-arg computed credit-used via raw `SELECT SUM(total)` for non-cancelled SOs, so paid credit customers never saw their limit free up (verified live: Test Credit customer, limit=30,000, SUM(total)=337,000, computed available=-307,000 regardless of payments). Migration splices the 17-arg live body via `pg_get_functiondef` — replaces the SUM(total) block with a call to `public.customer_credit_used(p_customer_id, NULL)`, the payments-aware helper `resubmit_sale_order` already uses. Then drops the dead 18-arg overload so the bug can't be reintroduced there. Idempotent (skips splice if body already references `customer_credit_used`). Post-fix audit reported 0 pending_approval SOs would auto-clear on resubmit today. Applied to staging (`mwvblpgbgxipvrevkeff`); `NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema'` fired so the RPC signature change lands immediately. Frontend unchanged. Removed from `docs/future-plans.md`.

- [2026-08-04] **Storage audit deferred 3C — storage cascade triggers** — 7 migrations: `20260804232123_storage_cleanup_infra.sql` (pg_net + supabase_vault extensions, `storage_cleanup_failures` audit table, `storage_delete_object(bucket, path, source_table, source_id)` helper) then per-table triggers `_customers`, `_companies_divisions`, `_inventory_items`, `_stock_adjustments`, `_consumption_entries`, `_landed_cost_lines`. AFTER DELETE nukes every referenced Storage object; AFTER UPDATE OF the path column nukes the OLD value. Array columns (`stock_adjustments.photo_urls`, `consumption_entries.attachments`) use `EXCEPT`-diff so only elements removed by the update are deleted. Helper normalises full public URLs to object paths + strips `?t=` cache-busters. Fire-and-forget — a Storage outage never aborts a DB write; failures land in `storage_cleanup_failures`. Vault secret must be created manually before triggers succeed (see `## 🔄 In Progress`). Plan: [docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-04-storage-cascade-triggers.md](docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-04-storage-cascade-triggers.md). Applied to staging.
- [2026-08-04] **Storage audit deferred 3B — server-side size + MIME limits on 4 legacy buckets** — `supabase/migrations/20260804225735_bucket_size_and_mime_limits.sql`. `inventory-item-photos`, `adjustment-photos`, `consumption-attachments`, `customer-credit-docs` previously had no server-side `file_size_limit` / `allowed_mime_types`, so client-side checks (file.size + accept attribute) were the only guard — bypassable by a direct `storage.upload` call. Now: all four capped at 10 MB with per-bucket MIME allow-lists (photos = jpeg/png/webp; consumption + credit-docs also allow application/pdf). Caps match existing per-dialog client-side checks. Applied to staging.
- [2026-08-04] **Storage audit deferred 3A — avatars bucket owner-check** — `supabase/migrations/20260804225530_avatars_owner_check.sql`. Before: any authenticated user could overwrite / delete any other user's avatar because insert/update/delete policies only checked `bucket_id = 'avatars'`. After: all three write policies now require `split_part(name, '.', 1) = auth.uid()::text`. Filename convention already encodes the uid (`${auth_user_id}.ext`, flat at bucket root — no folders, so `storage.foldername` isn't applicable). Public SELECT policy unchanged (avatars stay publicly readable). Applied to staging.
- [2026-08-04] **Storage audit — dirty-guard rollout to 8 upload dialogs** — `src/hooks/useDirtyDialogGuard.tsx` + wire-in across `CustomerDialog`, `InventoryItemFormDialog`, `ItemEditDialog`, `NewConsumptionDialog`, `WhAdjustmentDialog`, `CreateLcDialog` (inline in landed-costs page), `CompanyFormDialog`, `DivisionFormDialog`. Renders a shared `AlertDialog` ("Discard unsaved changes?" — Keep editing / Discard) when the user clicks outside / presses Escape / clicks Cancel with a dirty form. RHF dialogs pull `isDirty` from `form.formState.isDirty` + a `useWatch({control})` subscription so the outer render re-runs on every keystroke (without it the guard closure holds a stale `isDirty=false`). useState dialogs compute `isDirty` inline against defaults or seeded values. Successful submits bypass the guard via a `submittedRef` set right before close. 67 non-upload form dialogs across the app remain unpatched — deferred to a follow-up sweep.
- [2026-08-04] **Storage audit — orphan cleanup on cancel across 8 upload dialogs** — Same 8 files above + `src/hooks/useWarehouseOperations.ts`. Every dialog that uploads to Supabase Storage BEFORE the parent DB row is saved now tracks `sessionUploadsRef` and sweeps the bucket on cancel via `handleOpenChange` wrapper (routes cancel → sweep → parent close; skips sweep after successful save via `submittedRef`). Photo-replace within one session also drops the superseded upload immediately so a "Change photo" × 3 flow doesn't leak two orphans on save. Bucket coverage: `customer-credit-docs`, `inventory-item-photos`, `consumption-attachments`, `adjustment-photos`, `lc-bills`, `division-assets`.
- [2026-08-04] **Storage audit — landed-cost bill replace + consumption batch upload atomicity** — `src/app/(dashboard)/purchase/landed-costs/page.tsx` (bill replace reordered to upload-new → swap-state → best-effort delete-old; prior order deleted first, so a failed upload lost both) + `src/components/consumption/NewConsumptionDialog.tsx` (`Promise.all` → `Promise.allSettled` with rollback of successful uploads if any file failed — user asked for N, gets all-or-nothing instead of silent partial).
- [2026-08-04] **Storage audit — adjustment photos: store paths, not long-lived signed URLs** — `src/hooks/useWarehouseOperations.ts` (new `useAdjustmentPhotoSignedUrls(paths)` hook, 1-hour TTL mirroring `useBillSignedUrls`), `src/components/purchase/wh/WhAdjustmentDialog.tsx` (uploads store paths in `photo_urls`, MIME allow-list + 10 MB cap + extension fallback + rollback on partial upload failure), `src/components/purchase/wh/WhAdjustmentDetailDialog.tsx` + `WhAdjustmentsTab.tsx` (resolve paths on demand; pulse placeholder while signing; backwards-compatible with rows storing full URLs via `startsWith('http')`), `src/lib/queryKeys.ts` (new `adjustmentPhotoSignedUrls` key). Prior implementation persisted a 365-day signed URL to the DB = ~permanent access.

- [2026-08-04] **Category Attributes Phase 4 Task 4.2: `ProductAttributePicker` component** — `src/components/shared/ProductAttributePicker.tsx` (~290 LOC) — Guided item picker. State = `{selectedCategoryId, picks: Record<attribute_key, option_id>, pickHistory: string[]}`. History is the ordered list of picked keys — clearing an upper pick drops that key and every subsequent pick without needing sort_order client-side. Rendering: optional built-in category `Select` (skipped when `categoryFilter` prop is passed), current-picks chip row with per-chip clear-from-here `X`, then the picker body (from the RPC): next-attribute button row with per-option `item_count` badges, then a candidate items grid (`ItemPhoto` + name + SKU + one `Button` per brand-variant showing stock, colored green when > 0). Brand-variant click fires `onPick(itemId, brandVariantId)`. Amber "No items match — clear a pick above" banner when the pick set narrows to zero. Known limits deferred: chip labels currently show `attribute_key + option_id[0..6]` (label cache needed later); `warehouseScope` prop reserved but not wired (Task 5.4); no auto-select on single-item/single-variant (operator explicitly clicks in v1).
- [2026-08-04] **Category Attributes Phase 4 Task 4.1: `useAttributePickerStep` hook** — `src/hooks/useAttributes.ts` — TanStack Query wrapper around `rpc_attribute_picker_step`. Signature `(categoryId, picks)` where `picks: Record<attribute_key, option_id>`. Cache key includes `JSON.stringify(picks)` so distinct pick states cache independently; `staleTime: 30s` balances freshness against duplicate requests within a picker session. Returns typed `PickerStepResult` = `{items, next_attribute, next_options}`. Falls back to an empty result shape on null data so the caller renders without extra guarding.
- [2026-08-04] **Category Attributes Phase 3 Task 3.3: item-row chip strip** — `src/components/shared/AttributeChipStrip.tsx`, `src/components/services/inventory/ItemRow.tsx` — Compact read-only strip under each item name showing `label: value` chips for every attribute pick. Batches the option lookups via a single `useOptionsForDefinitionsBatch` query that pulls every option row for the row's effective definitions in one request and buckets by `definition_id` — avoids the N-per-row query fan-out that a naive per-row `useAttributeOptionsForDefinition` would cause across a list view. Up to 4 chips shown inline (maxChips prop) with `+N more` overflow tail. Keeps the picked value displayed even after the underlying attribute or option is archived. Silent (renders nothing) when the item has no picks.
- [2026-08-04] **Category Attributes Phase 3 Task 3.2 + 3.1b: ItemAttributesSection + wire into ItemEditDialog + dead-hook cleanup** — `src/components/master-data/attributes/ItemAttributesSection.tsx`, `src/components/services/inventory/ItemEditDialog.tsx`, `src/hooks/useInventory.ts`, `src/lib/queryKeys.ts` — New shared section renders every effective attribute for the item's category as a labeled Select over active options; `X` button clears a pick (parent then deletes the row). **Race-guard:** the section only starts propagating desired-state to the parent once `useItemAttributes.isSuccess` is true — a mid-fetch Save would otherwise push an all-null payload and silently wipe every existing attribute row. New-item flow bypasses the guard (nothing to hydrate). Renders currently-picked option even if archived post-pick (with `(archived)` suffix). Legacy "chips" free-form UI removed from `ItemEditDialog` — the `setChips([])` on every open meant the UI silently wiped chips on save anyway. `useUpsertInventoryItemAttributes` hook + `queryKeys.inventory.itemAttributes` key deleted (writing to a column shape that never existed on the current schema). `handleSubmit` switched to async: awaits item create/update, then awaits attribute upsert; failure at either step surfaces as a toast and keeps the dialog open. Empty `attrValues` skips the upsert entirely (never issues a no-op DELETE).
- [2026-08-04] **Category Attributes Phase 3 Task 3.1a: item-value hooks (safe additive slice)** — `src/hooks/useAttributes.ts` — Added `useItemAttributes(itemId)` (reads current picks) and `useUpsertItemAttributes()` (mutation accepting FULL desired state; `option_id: null` → delete row, set → upsert on `(item_id, definition_id)`). Definitions absent from the payload are left untouched, so callers only need to send rows they own. - [2026-08-04] **Category Attributes Phase 1 Task 1.5: `rpc_attribute_picker_step` RPC — one-round-trip picker step** — `supabase/migrations/20260804120400_picker_step_rpc.sql`, `src/types/database.types.ts` — Signature `(p_category_id uuid, p_picks jsonb DEFAULT '{}')` returning jsonb. Given a category and current pick set, returns three-in-one: `items` (remaining candidates with active brand_variants), `next_attribute` (first effective attribute by `sort_order` not yet picked, only when >1 candidate remains — else null so the caller stops asking), and `next_options` (options for `next_attribute` that at least one candidate actually holds, each with `item_count`). Match semantics: an item is disqualified only when it holds a non-matching value for a picked attribute — items with no value on that attribute stay eligible ("unknown / any"), so items with sparse attribute coverage aren't unfairly filtered out. Definition lookup walks both subtree AND ancestor chain (depth cap 10) so an attribute defined at an ancestor category still applies to descendants. STABLE + SECURITY INVOKER + pinned `search_path`. First cut ships as-is; perf-tune after real data lands per plan §Phase 1.5. **Phase 1 (DB layer) is now complete** — three tables + two RPCs on staging. Ready for Phase 2 (definition editor UI).
- [2026-08-04] **Category Attributes Phase 1 Task 1.4: `get_effective_attributes(category_id)` RPC** — `supabase/migrations/20260804120300_effective_attributes_function.sql`, `src/types/database.types.ts` — Returns the effective attribute schema for a category: union of definitions on `(category_id + all ancestors)` walked via recursive CTE (depth cap 10). Returns rows `(definition_id, category_id, category_name, attribute_key, label_en, label_ar, sort_order, depth, is_inherited)` ordered by `sort_order ASC, depth ASC` — sort_order primary, ancestors first as tie-break. `is_inherited = (depth > 0)`. STABLE + SECURITY INVOKER so the caller's JWT drives RLS on the underlying tables. `search_path` pinned to `public, pg_catalog`. Applied to staging.
- [2026-08-04] **Category Attributes Phase 1 Task 1.3: `inventory_item_attributes` table — per-item picked value** — `supabase/migrations/20260804120200_item_attributes_table.sql`, `src/types/database.types.ts` — Links `inventory_items` to `inventory_attribute_definitions` + `inventory_attribute_options`. UNIQUE `(item_id, definition_id)` — one picked value per attribute per item. `ON DELETE RESTRICT` on `option_id` so archived-but-in-use options cannot be hard-deleted (soft-hide via `is_archived` is the retirement path). Cleared values delete the row rather than storing NULL — no distinction between "never set" and "cleared". Indexes on `item_id`, `definition_id`, `option_id`. RLS on with authenticated read/write. `set_updated_at()` trigger. Applied to staging.
- [2026-08-04] **Category Attributes Phase 1 Task 1.2: `inventory_attribute_options` table with `is_archived` soft-hide** — `supabase/migrations/20260804120100_attribute_options_table.sql`, `src/types/database.types.ts` — Columns: `id`, `definition_id → inventory_attribute_definitions`, `value_en`, `value_ar`, `sort_order`, `is_archived` (default false), `created_at`. Case-insensitive UNIQUE `(definition_id, lower(value_en))` prevents "Copper" and "COPPER" duplicates. Partial index `WHERE NOT is_archived` for fast picker lookups (active options only). RLS on with authenticated read/write. Soft-hide via `is_archived` so items already using an option keep their reference when the option is retired. Applied to staging.
- [2026-08-04] **Category Attributes Phase 1 Task 1.1: `inventory_attribute_definitions` table + branch-uniqueness trigger** — `supabase/migrations/20260804120000_attribute_definitions_table.sql`, `src/types/database.types.ts` — Category-level attribute schema. Columns: `id`, `category_id → inventory_categories`, `attribute_key`, `label_en`, `label_ar`, `sort_order`, `created_at`, `updated_at`, `created_by → user_data`. UNIQUE `(category_id, attribute_key)` + BEFORE INSERT/UPDATE trigger `_check_attribute_key_branch_unique()` that walks ancestors + descendants recursively (depth cap 10) and raises `23505` if the same key already exists anywhere in the top-level tree. Indexes on `category_id` and `attribute_key`. RLS on with authenticated read/write policies. Trigger uses `public.set_updated_at()` (project convention — `touch_updated_at` from the plan spec does not exist here). Applied to staging (`mwvblpgbgxipvrevkeff`); dev DB frozen until `deploy/warehouse-shipping` ships.
- [2026-08-04] **Consumption granularity — 3 narrower `.create` keys + contextual dialog filter + auto-picked consumer** — `src/lib/permissions.ts`, `src/components/master-data/PermissionTree.tsx`, `src/hooks/usePermissions.ts`, `src/components/consumption/NewConsumptionDialog.tsx`, `src/app/(dashboard)/warehouse/custody/page.tsx`, `src/app/(dashboard)/consumption/page.tsx`. Added three narrower keys `consumption.create.team` / `consumption.create.place` / `consumption.create.internal` alongside the umbrella `consumption.create` (retained as an "all three" alias for backwards compat). Two new helpers in `usePermissions.ts`: `useCanCreateConsumptionFor(kind)` returns true if the caller has system.admin OR the umbrella OR the narrower key for that kind; `useCanCreateAnyConsumption()` returns true if the caller can create at least one consumer type — used to decide whether to render the New Consumption button at all. `NewConsumptionDialog` gains a `restrictConsumerTypes?: ConsumerType[]` prop; the Consumer segmented control now shows the intersection of caller-perm-allowed and caller-passed restriction, and hides itself entirely when only one type is available (the sub-picker below already implies the type — no wasted vertical space). Custody Team card passes `restrictConsumerTypes={['team']}`; Place card passes `['place']`; `/consumption` header passes nothing (all three). Custody card's Consume button now gates on `useCanCreateConsumptionFor(kind)` (not the umbrella) so a "team-only" role sees Consume on Team cards but not on Place cards. `/consumption` page's New button gates on `useCanCreateAnyConsumption()`. When opened from a Custody card the consumer sub is pre-selected to the same sub as the source (Team 2's custody feeds Team 2 as consumer) — operator only needs to click Confirm. UI-only gate for now — `rpc_post_consumption` doesn't check the narrower keys; server-side enforcement is a follow-up migration. Total permission keys 117 → 121.

Extends the 2026-07-08 inventory importer so bulk-loaded items land with routing defaults (`inventory_items.default_warehouse_id` + `default_sub_container_id`, both nullable + partial indexes; informational — receival/delivery dialogs MAY pre-fill from them). **Template:** three category columns by default (`Category 1/2/3`); parser discovers `Category N` columns by regex + sorts by numeric suffix so operators can add `Category 4`, `Category 5`, … for deeper trees, and trailing-empty columns collapse per row (`Category 3` blank → 2-level hierarchy for that row).
