- [2026-06-25] **Supabase Perf Cleanup Wave 3 — consolidate 9 overlapping permissive policies + `warehouse_stock_view` SECURITY INVOKER** — `supabase/migrations/20260625120000_perf_consolidate_overlapping_policies.sql`. Lint 0006 flagged 9 tables where two permissive policies fired on the same role+action; 7 had identical USING(true) bodies on both, so the redundant FOR SELECT policy was dropped (employee_services, installed_products, tool_assignments, service_customers, service_customer_phones, service_customer_addresses, service_instructions). `service_brands` "Manage services write" FOR ALL policy split into per-action INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE so the manage gate (master_data.services.manage permission) no longer also fires on every SELECT — change-request approval workflow on service_change_requests is independent and unaffected. `invoices` two UPDATE policies (open non-void + admin-only void) merged into a single policy preserving the exact combined semantics: anyone can update to non-void, only invoices.manage can set status='void'. `warehouse_stock_view` switched from SECURITY DEFINER to SECURITY INVOKER via `ALTER VIEW … SET (security_invoker = true)` — all underlying tables already grant authenticated SELECT, so callers see the same data without the bypass risk.

- [2026-06-25] **PO Version Tabs Redesign — per-stage snapshots + segmented tab UI** — `supabase/migrations/20260625100000_po_versions_stage_column.sql` (TRUNCATE po_versions, add `stage text NOT NULL CHECK (stage IN ('rfq','draft','po'))`, replace old `(po_id, version_number)` unique with per-stage `(po_id, stage, version_number)`; only the snapshot table is affected — purchase_orders, receivals, bills, inventory_movements, FIFO layers, sales are all untouched), `src/lib/poVersionHelper.ts` (`savePoSnapshot` now takes `Stage` and computes per-stage version_number via `.eq('po_id', poId).eq('stage', stage)`; new `stageOf(POType): Stage` helper exported), `src/hooks/usePurchaseOrders.ts` (snapshot on every `useCreatePO` and `useUpdatePO` save with `stageOf(po_type)`; `useSubmitPOForApproval` now writes the `'po'`-stage snapshot — submission is when PO-v1 is born; `useSubmitPoVersion` replaced its raw `po_versions` INSERT with a `savePoSnapshot(..., 'po')` call (the raw insert was crashing on the new `stage NOT NULL` constraint); `useSavePoAsDraft` newly takes a per-stage snapshot at the end so Save-as-Draft shows up in the tab UI; `PoVersion` type gains `stage: 'rfq' | 'draft' | 'po'`), `src/hooks/usePOApprovals.ts` (removed all 3 `savePoSnapshot('approved')` calls — approval is a status change on existing po-v1, not a content change), `src/components/purchase/PoVersionTabs.tsx` (full rewrite — two-row segmented UI with state tabs row 1, version chips row 2, ✓ marker on live-state tab, orange Current chip on live tab only, empty-state copy per stage), `src/components/purchase/PoDetailDialog.tsx` (state shape changed from `activeVersionTab:number` to `{activeStage:Stage, activeVersion:number|null}`; switch logic uses the new shape; tab strip always renders now, not just when `versions.length > 0`), `src/app/(dashboard)/purchase/edit-po/[id]/page.tsx` (matching `(activeStage, activeVersionNumber)` state at four call sites: initial useEffect, restore handler, delete-version handler, and the PoVersionTabs prop pass; `handleSaveDraft` now `router.push('/purchase/orders')` after the toast so Save-as-Draft exits the page like Submit does). Existing POs after the TRUNCATE migration show RFQ(0)/Draft(0)/PO(0) until the next edit/submit writes a snapshot. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-25-po-version-tabs-redesign-design.md`. Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-25-po-version-tabs-redesign.md`.

- [2026-06-24] **PO Approvals — one-click Force Approve for owners + role-slug case-mismatch fix** — `src/hooks/usePOApprovals.ts` (made `forceComment` optional in `useForceApproveAllSteps`, dropped the mandatory-comment guard, rewrote the activity log to write one `Force Approved: <Role>` entry per step instead of a single consolidated entry — keeps the trailing `PO Fully Approved (Force)` summary; **fixed pre-existing case mismatch** in `useMyApprovalRoles` by normalising DB role names (`'Owner'`, `'Purchase Manager'`) to the lowercase snake_case slug format that matches `po_approvals.role` and every existing comparison site), `src/app/(dashboard)/purchase/approvals/page.tsx` (replaced the multi-step Force Approve dialog with an inline shadcn `AlertDialog` confirm popover summarising the role count + names, added separate `handleForceApprove` function, stripped every `'force'`-mode branch from `openDialog`, the bottom dialog header/body/footer, and the `ApprovalDialogState` interface — those branches were always dead code beyond the now-deleted force-mode entry point). The role-slug fix means the existing `myRoles.includes('owner')` visibility check and the own-step preference inside `openDialog` finally fire correctly; without it, the Force Approve button would never have shown for anyone.

- [2026-06-24] **Supabase Perf Cleanup Wave 2: rewrite 14 RLS policies to wrap `auth.<fn>()` in `(SELECT …)`** — `supabase/migrations/20260624110000_perf_rls_init_plan.sql` (DROP + CREATE for all 14 policies flagged by Supabase lint `0003_auth_rls_initplan`: `service_brands."Manage services write service_brands"`, `traccar_geofences` × 4 (read/insert/update/delete), `user_ui_preferences` × 3 (self_select/self_upsert/self_update), `service_change_requests.scr_select`, `purge_batches."Admins read purge batches"`, `team_live_locations` × 2 (tll_insert/tll_update), `chat_messages.chat_messages_insert_strict`, `follow_up_requests.fur_insert`). Every `auth.uid()` and `auth.role()` call now wrapped as `(SELECT auth.uid())` / `(SELECT auth.role())` so Postgres evaluates them once per query as a stable initplan instead of recomputing per row. Pure evaluation-order change — same row visibility for the same users, just faster scans on these tables. Migration applied via `npx supabase db push`.

- [2026-06-24] **Supabase Perf Cleanup Wave 1: drop duplicate indexes + conditional `backfill_conversation_last_messages` call** — `supabase/migrations/20260624100000_perf_drop_duplicate_indexes.sql` (DROP INDEX `chat_messages_conv_created_idx` and `idx_tsa_team` — both were identical duplicates of `idx_chat_messages_conversation_created` / `idx_team_sched_team_id`; kept the descriptive `idx_<table>_<col>` names. Wastes-no-writes cleanup flagged by Supabase lint `0009_duplicate_index`), `src/app/api/wati/sync-contacts/route.ts` (made the `backfill_conversation_last_messages` RPC call conditional — runs a cheap `head:true count:'exact' limit:1` on `chat_conversations WHERE last_message IS NULL AND last_message_at IS NOT NULL` first; only calls the RPC when there's at least one candidate row. The RPC scans the whole chat_conversations × chat_messages join, so skipping it when there's nothing to do cuts ~77 ms × every empty sync — the query-performance CSV showed it being called 1119 times). Migration applied via `npx supabase db push`.

- [2026-06-24] **Order Quotations — list-page polish + dedicated detail/edit pages + server-side PDF + iframe preview** — `src/app/(dashboard)/quotations/page.tsx` (rewritten to mirror the orders list — `PageContainer`, compact desktop search + Filters popover, mobile sticky bar + bottom Sheet, active-state count badges, active filter chip row with X-to-remove, infinite scroll via IntersectionObserver, quick-search auto-detect for phone vs quotation number; card click navigates to `/quotations/[id]` instead of opening a side sheet), `src/app/(dashboard)/quotations/[id]/page.tsx` (new — dedicated detail page with back button, status badge, customer subtitle, Edit & Send / Edit & Resend button, Preview / Logs tabs), `src/app/(dashboard)/quotations/[id]/edit/page.tsx` (new — wraps `useCreateQuotation(initialDraft)` and reuses `QuotationFormPanel`; same Save Draft / Send via Wati / Send via WHAPI buttons as create page; `save_order_quotation` RPC already `ON CONFLICT (quotation_id) DO UPDATE` so edit lands cleanly with no DB change), `src/hooks/useQuotations.ts` (`useQuery` → `useInfiniteQuery` with PAGE_SIZE=50, joins `service_customers(name, service_customer_phones(phone))` not `customers(...)` — fixes "— —" on list cards because quotations link via `service_customer_id`, not `customer_id`), `src/hooks/useQuotationDetail.ts` (same FK fix), `src/hooks/useCreateQuotation.ts` (accepts optional `initialDraft`; broadened cache invalidation to also invalidate `quotation-detail` + `quotation-counts`; `sendViaWati`/`sendViaWhapi` no longer take an `HTMLElement` — they save the draft, POST to `/api/quotations/[id]/generate-pdf` for the URL, then forward to Wati/WHAPI), `src/components/quotations/QuotationListCard.tsx` (uses `<Link prefetch>` not `router.push` so Next.js compiles the detail route before the click and multi-clicks dedupe), `src/components/quotations/QuotationPdfPreviewIframe.tsx` (new — debounced `<iframe srcDoc>` posting to `/api/quotations/preview-html`; ignores stale responses; accepts optional `issuingDate`/`validUntilDate` overrides so saved quotations display their original dates), `src/lib/quotations/quotation-pdf-html.ts` (new — pure HTML builder mirroring `confirmation-pdf-html.ts`; bilingual layout, RTL services + totals tables, notes block, "Valid for N days" footnote), `src/lib/quotations/generate-quotation-pdf.ts` (new — server-side Puppeteer renderer with per-process asset/font cache; fetches `order_quotations` + line items + `service_customers` + phones; uploads to `quotation-pdfs` bucket), `src/app/api/quotations/[id]/generate-pdf/route.ts` (new — Bearer-token auth, `runtime='nodejs'`, returns `{ url, storageKey, bytes }`), `src/app/api/quotations/preview-html/route.ts` (new — same auth, accepts draft body, returns the same HTML the PDF will use), `src/lib/queryKeys.ts` (`quotations.list` now `['quotations','list',filter]` to dodge stale `useQuery` cache from before the infinite-query switch). Deleted `src/components/quotations/QuotationPdfPreview.tsx` (374 lines of React PDF mockup), `src/components/quotations/QuotationDetailSheet.tsx` (dead since detail page took over), `src/lib/quotations/capture-pdf.ts` (html2canvas + jspdf wrapper). DOM-screenshot pipeline replaced with server-side Puppeteer — PDFs now vector/searchable, iframe preview is pixel-identical to the customer PDF, route bundle is dramatically lighter (no html2canvas, no jspdf, no 374-line preview component on the client).

- [2026-06-22] **Contact Centre — Conversation list + chat header use CRM name → phone only** — `src/lib/contact-center/local/schema.ts` (`LocalConversation` gets a denormalised `customer_name` field), `src/lib/contact-center/local/repos/conversations.ts` (lazyFetch joins `service_customers` via `customer_id_v2` and flattens `name` into `customer_name` before bulkPut so Dexie stays flat and the list doesn't N+1), `src/components/contact-center/v2/ChatListV2.tsx` (title + avatar initial chain is now `customer_name ?? wati_phone ?? 'Unknown'` — WhatsApp display name dropped), `src/components/contact-center/v2/ContactCenterSidebarV2.tsx` (chat-detail header header same — `customer?.name ?? activePhone ?? 'Unknown'`). Without this, customers with a `service_customers` row were still shown under whichever WhatsApp push name they had set, so the conversation list disagreed with the customer view.

- [2026-06-22] **Contact Centre — WHAPI outbound text + media end-to-end (images, video, audio, documents)** — `src/lib/contact-center/local/sync-worker.ts` (sendText now branches on per-message provider and POSTs `/api/whapi/send-message` with `skipDbInsert:true` for WHAPI; new parallel WHAPI branch on sendFile uploads to chat-attachments then sends with the right URL field by mime family — `imageUrl`/`videoUrl`/`audioUrl`/`documentUrl`; voice notes are `audio/ogg` → `/messages/audio`; WATI sendText + sendTemplate now always promote the local row to `delivery_status='sent'` after a successful invoke — `external_id` is still only written when the wamid is actually present, but the bubble no longer sticks at the clock icon forever when the response shape omits it), `src/lib/contact-center/local/mutations.ts` (`sendFileLocal` accepts `provider` and tags the optimistic row with the matching source; both `sendFileLocal` and `sendMessageLocal` carry `provider` through the pending-write payload), `src/components/contact-center/v2/ContactCenterSidebarV2.tsx` (passes `provider` into `handleSendFile`, `handleVoiceNote`, and `chatMessages.sendSessionMessage`). Solves "image/video/document never reaches the customer" — sync-worker.sendFile only had a WATI branch, so WHAPI file uploads were silently sent through `api-wati`.

- [2026-06-22] **Contact Centre — WHAPI source separation + from_me echo handling + sent:false hardening** — `src/app/api/whapi/webhook/route.ts` (inbound rows now tagged `source='whatsapp_whapi'` not `'whatsapp_api'` — without this every WHAPI inbound landed under the WATI divider in the UnifiedThread; webhook regular-message path now respects `msg.from_me` — echoed agent messages route to `msg.chat_id` instead of `msg.from` so the conversation lookup doesn't create a ghost row keyed on our own business number, and the row inserts as `from_type='agent'` with `delivery_status='sent'` and no `unread_count` bump; reaction-target diagnostic — `console.warn` when `action.target` doesn't match any `chat_messages.external_id` so silent no-ops are visible), `src/app/api/whapi/fetch-messages/route.ts` (history backfill also tagged `'whatsapp_whapi'`), `src/app/api/whapi/send-message/route.ts` (conversation lookup filtered by `provider='whapi'` and the new-conversation insert sets `provider:'whapi'` so outbound WHAPI sends never land their `conversation_id` on a WATI conversation row; route now checks WHAPI's `sent` boolean — `sent:false` maps to HTTP 502 instead of optimistically marking the bubble 'sent'; full response body logged for diagnostics; outbound row tagged `'whatsapp_whapi'`), `supabase/migrations/20260622130000_whapi_messages_source_backfill.sql` (re-tags every existing row whose conversation `provider='whapi'` from `whatsapp_api` → `whatsapp_whapi`).

- [2026-06-22] **Contact Centre — Provider auto-switch must not fight a manual toggle** — `src/hooks/contact-center/useProviderSuggest.ts` (moved the `dismissedAt === last.id` short-circuit to the top of the effect before the typing check, and stamp `last.id` on the no-typing auto-switch path too). The effect re-fires on every render; previously after one auto-switch the dismissedAt guard was only checked inside the "user is typing" branch, so a manual provider toggle immediately got fought by the next render. After this fix the effect goes silent for an inbound message once it's handled once, regardless of subsequent typing or manual toggles.

- [2026-06-22] **Contact Centre — WATI parallel-webhook dedup via advisory-lock RPC** — `supabase/migrations/20260622103234_chat_messages_dedup_rpc.sql` (new `cc_dedup_insert_message` function guarded by `pg_advisory_xact_lock` keyed on `hashtext(conversation_id || ':' || from_type || ':' || text)` — the second parallel call blocks until the first commits, then its dedup SELECT sees the row and short-circuits to a backfill instead of inserting again), `supabase/migrations/20260622110000_chat_messages_dedup_rpc_source_cast.sql` (re-recreates the function with an explicit `text → message_source` cast on INSERT; the first version's implicit cast broke with SQLSTATE 42804), `src/app/api/wati/webhook/route.ts` (collapsed 189 lines of JS dedup heuristics — exact external_id, agent optimistic-row match, broad text-based dedup, wamid dedup — into a single RPC call). WATI fires `sessionMessageSent` + `sessionMessageSent_v2` for one logical message within ~50 ms; the previous JS-side dedup caught serial firings, but two Next.js handlers running in parallel both missed each other's not-yet-committed INSERT and inserted side-by-side duplicates.

- [2026-06-22] **Contact Centre — "Delete for Everyone" support** — `supabase/migrations/20260621220000_chat_messages_revoked_at.sql` (new `revoked_at timestamptz` column + partial index; distinct from `deleted_at` so revoked rows stay visible as placeholders instead of being filtered out by the purge soft-delete UI logic), `src/lib/contact-center/local/db.ts` (Dexie bumped to v2 with upgrade hook backfilling `revoked_at: null` on existing rows), `src/lib/contact-center/local/schema.ts` + `src/types/contact-center.ts` (`revoked_at` field added to `LocalMessage` / `ChatMessage`), `src/app/api/whapi/webhook/route.ts` (statuses event with `status: 'deleted'` → stamps `revoked_at` + clears `text`/`attachments`, keeps reactions), `src/components/contact-center/v2/MessageBubble.tsx` (muted italic 🚫 placeholder — "You deleted this message" / "This message was deleted", react button suppressed), `src/hooks/contact-center/useLiveThread.ts` (3 select clauses now include `revoked_at`). WATI handler intentionally not implemented — Meta's Business API hides "Delete for Everyone" in both directions on Business-side chats, documented in the WATI webhook with a TODO comment.

- [2026-06-22] **Contact Centre — Reactions end-to-end (5 bugs across WATI + WHAPI)** — `src/app/api/whapi/webhook/route.ts` (WHAPI receive: detect `type='action'` with `action.type='reaction'`; was hardcoded to `type='reaction'` so every customer reaction landed as a `[action]` ghost row), `src/app/api/whapi/send-reaction/route.ts` (rewrote — WHAPI's contract is `PUT /messages/<id>/reaction` body `{emoji}`, was `POST /messages/reaction` body `{to,messageId,emoji}` → 4xx on every send), `supabase/functions/api-wati/index.ts` + `supabase/migrations/20260621210000_chat_messages_wati_id.sql` (WATI send: undocumented `sendReaction` wants WATI's MongoDB ObjectID not the wamid; webhook captures `body.id` into new `chat_messages.wati_id`, edge function looks it up by external_id/wamid with `getMessages` fallback + in-memory cache for legacy messages), both webhook reaction handlers (`from_type` attribution: WATI echoes the agent's own reactions back with `owner:true`, WHAPI with `from_me:true` — was hard-coding `'customer'` for every event, overwriting the agent's Dexie row so the emoji visually "disappeared"), webhook idempotency (WATI fires `sessionMessageSent` AND `sessionMessageSent_v2` for the same reaction — flipped semantics to state-based so duplicates no-op instead of toggling off), `src/lib/contact-center/local/mutations.ts` + `src/hooks/contact-center/useChatMessages.ts` (one-reaction-per-sender: picking a different emoji REPLACES the agent's prior one instead of stacking; toggle-off sends empty emoji upstream so customer's WhatsApp clears), `src/lib/contact-center/local/sync-worker.ts` (removed silent 4xx swallow — was `return` ing on 4xx and deleting the pending_write as if successful; now throws `TerminalError` with structured browser-console logs).

- [2026-06-22] **Contact Centre — WHAPI media quota fix (mirror to Supabase Storage)** — `src/lib/whapi/store-media.ts` (new `buildAttachmentSkeleton` + `mirrorWhapiMedia`; webhook inserts immediately with the proxy URL for instant chat delivery, then `after()` downloads the file from `gate.whapi.cloud` once and UPDATEs the row with a permanent `chat-attachments/whapi/<phone>/<externalId>_<kind>.<ext>` Supabase URL — Realtime swaps the URL in the open chat without the user noticing), `src/app/api/whapi/webhook/route.ts` + `src/app/api/whapi/fetch-messages/route.ts` (wired the skeleton + mirror pattern into both inbound paths), `src/app/api/whapi/media/route.ts` (legacy proxy gets `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` so old DB rows still pointing at the proxy at least cache after first fetch), `supabase/migrations/20260621200000_relax_chat_attachments_mime_and_size.sql` (dropped the bucket's mime allow-list — was rejecting CSV/ZIP/`application/octet-stream` and silently falling back to the proxy URL, defeating the mirror — and raised file_size_limit to 100 MB to match WhatsApp's own document cap). Net effect: every inbound media file hits WHAPI exactly once, ever; subsequent renders serve from Supabase CDN.

- [2026-06-21] **Contact Centre — Wati customer-message duplication fix + dedup index** — `src/app/api/wati/webhook/route.ts` (extended the broad text-based dedup that previously only covered agent messages to BOTH directions — matches by `conversation_id + from_type + text + created_at within 2 min`; backfills canonical `external_id` / `wamid` on the surviving row), `src/app/api/wati/fetch-messages/route.ts` (mirrored guard with a wider ±5 min window — V1 `useLiveThread` still calls this route on chat-open even though V2 sidebar is active, so it was the second insertion path producing duplicates), `supabase/migrations/20260621180000_chat_messages_dedup_index.sql` (composite partial index `(conversation_id, from_type, created_at DESC, text) WHERE message_kind = 'message'` — drops the dedup lookup from a sequential scan to ~1-5 ms). Root cause: Wati fires more than one webhook for the same logical WhatsApp message — numeric Wati id on the first firing, `wamid.*` on the second — so external_id/wamid checks missed and a duplicate row landed. Fix prevents new duplicates; existing duplicated rows stay until a future merge cleanup.

- [2026-06-21] **Contact Centre — Sidebar UX polish (auto-collapse + agent-chatting pill)** — `src/components/contact-center/v2/DialPad.tsx` (default open state flipped to `false` — opening a chat no longer drops the dial pad in the user's face), `src/components/contact-center/v2/SectionAccordion.tsx` (dropped the localStorage-backed cross-chat persistence — was working against the "start collapsed every time" intent the user wanted), `src/components/contact-center/v2/ContactCenterSidebarV2.tsx` (DialPad + accordion container both keyed by `activeConversationId` so they remount with fresh closed state on every chat switch), `src/components/contact-center/v2/ChatListV2.tsx` (replaced the always-on `assigned_agent` label under the customer name with an ephemeral "[agent] chatting" pill positioned above the timestamp; pill shows only when `last_message_from_type === 'agent'` AND `assigned_agent` set AND `last_message_at` < 30 min old; list re-renders every 60 s via a `now` state so the pill auto-clears without a reload). Unread-count behaviour unchanged: webhook already increments only on inbound customer messages.

- [2026-06-21] **Contact Centre Access toggle + 3CX Extension on User dialogs** — `supabase/migrations/20260621100000_profiles_has_contact_centre_access.sql` (adds `profiles.has_contact_centre_access` boolean default false, backfills `true` for users whose role grants `contact_centre.view` so day-1 behaviour is identical), `src/app/api/users/[id]/route.ts` + `src/app/api/users/create/route.ts` (accept `has_contact_centre_access` and `threecx_extension` 2-8 digits; duplicate-extension surfaces as a clean 409 "already assigned" message; create route rolls back the orphan auth user on duplicate so the admin can retry), `src/hooks/useProfiles.ts` (extended `useCreateUser`/`useUpdateUser` payload types), `src/components/contact-center/ContactCenterSidebarGate.tsx` (gate accepts EITHER the new flag OR the `contact_centre.view` role permission — additive, no regressions), `src/components/master-data/EditUserDialog.tsx` + `src/components/master-data/AddUserDialog.tsx` (replaced the two flat toggle cards with two grouped section cards — "Teams Operation Control" with Users2 icon containing Team Leader + linked-employee select + Division Manager; "Contact Centre" with Headphones icon containing CC Access toggle + 3CX Extension input that only renders when access is on; 2-8 digit client + API validation; toggling access off keeps the extension stored so re-enabling preserves it), `src/app/(dashboard)/master-data/admin/contact-centre-extensions/page.tsx` (new — modern redesign: header with Headphones tile, 3 stat tiles Total/With Access/With Extension, search by name/email/extension, per-user card row with avatar initials, inline access toggle, extension input disabled while access off, check/x save bar that only appears when row is dirty), `src/components/master-data/AdminSidebar.tsx` (new "Contact Centre" section with Extensions link gated by `master_data.users.manage`), `src/app/(dashboard)/admin/contact-centre/extensions/page.tsx` deleted (moved). The standalone admin page and the Edit User dialog now both write the same column; CC access is no longer hidden behind a role permission tick.

- [2026-06-21] **Contact Centre — Inline Inbound Call Strip** — `src/components/contact-center/v2/InboundCallStrip.tsx` (new — single-line row component: circular phone icon with ping ring, stacked name + number/duration, pill `Open chat` + round red decline/hangup icon, amber/green gradient + left-edge accent), `src/components/contact-center/v2/ContactCenterSidebarV2.tsx` (mounts strip between header and DialPad in list + detail views; auto-expand effect: when calls.length > 0 AND sidebarView === 'collapsed', force `setCcSidebar('expanded') + expandSidebar()` so the row is always visible when a call rings; single shared `useLivePolledInboundCalls()` instance feeds both the strip and the auto-expand effect — avoids duplicate polls), `src/app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx` (removed `<InboundCallBanner />` mount), `src/components/contact-center/v2/InboundCallBanner.tsx` deleted (no remaining imports). Replaces the floating top-right `+974…` popup with a sidebar-integrated row that matches the rest of the contact-centre UI.

- [2026-06-20] **Wati inbound webhook — add shared-secret check** — `src/app/api/wati/webhook/route.ts` now calls `verifySharedSecret(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get('secret'), WATI_WEBHOOK_SECRET)` at the top of POST. Without this any anonymous caller who knew the URL could post arbitrary inbound-message payloads (every other inbound webhook — 3CX, 17track, Dibsy, WhAPI — already validated itself). First pass used the `x-webhook-secret` header pattern (matching WhAPI) but the Wati dashboard exposes only URL / Status / Events — no custom-header field — so the gate was reworked to read from a `?secret=…` query string instead (matching the 3CX pattern). Production rollout = (1) set `WATI_WEBHOOK_SECRET` in env, (2) Wati dashboard → Edit Webhook → append `?secret=<WATI_WEBHOOK_SECRET>` to the URL. Fails open when the env var is empty so deploying doesn't break dev. No `.env.example` exists in the repo so a documented placeholder was added to `.env.local` only.

- [2026-06-20] **Middleware webhook bypass — silences refresh_token_not_found spam** — `src/middleware.ts` now declares a `WEBHOOK_PREFIXES` allowlist (`/api/3cx/webhook/`, `/api/wati/webhook/`, `/api/whapi/webhook/`, `/api/webhooks/`, `/api/payments/dibsy/webhook/`) and short-circuits before `supabase.auth.getUser()` for any matching path. Third-party inbound webhooks never carry a Supabase session cookie, so the previous unconditional `getUser()` always entered the `@supabase/ssr` refresh-token path and logged four `AuthApiError: Invalid Refresh Token: Refresh Token Not Found` lines per call (visible after every 3CX hit). Each gated route validates its own shared secret / HMAC signature (Wati doesn't — flagged as a follow-up task; not a regression since middleware never gated it either). Also corrected an outdated comment in `src/app/api/3cx/call/make/route.ts` that referenced the previously-non-existent `WEBHOOK_PREFIXES` constant.

- [2026-06-20] **Country Codes admin page** — `src/components/master-data/CountryCodesAdmin.tsx` (list-and-edit UI for the `country_codes` table powering `PhoneInputWithCode` across the app: add-row form with name / dial code / ISO / flag, code normalised to `+N`, inline `is_active` toggle, `sort_order` column), `src/app/(dashboard)/master-data/admin/country-codes/page.tsx` (page wrapper), `src/components/master-data/AdminSidebar.tsx` (new "Country Codes" nav item with Globe icon under `master_data.admin.view`), `supabase/migrations/20260620150000_country_codes_write_policies.sql` (INSERT + UPDATE policies for authenticated users, matching the pattern used for currencies and payment_methods), `supabase/migrations/20260620150100_country_codes_seq_align.sql` (idempotent `setval()` so the SERIAL sequence catches up to MAX(id) — needed where rows were seeded with explicit IDs). Both migrations already applied to prod and staging.

- [2026-06-20] **Approval Settings — Backfill legacy slug names in chain tiers** — `supabase/migrations/20260620153000_normalize_approval_chain_tier_roles.sql` rewrites `approval_chain_tiers.required_roles` from the old enum slugs (`purchase_manager`, `accountant`, `owner`) to the current `custom_roles.name` Title-Case values (`Purchase Manager`, etc.). The slug→name map is recovered from `workflow_approval_steps.step_key → role_id → custom_roles.name`, so unmapped values are preserved via `COALESCE`. Before this, every existing tier rendered amber-warning chips with raw slug text because the UI's coverage/color lookups compare against `custom_roles.name`.

- [2026-06-20] **Approval Settings — Scope to PO workflow steps + drop Role Assignments tab** — `src/components/purchase/ApprovalChainsTab.tsx` (joined `useWorkflowSteps` to derive the role list — only roles bound to an ACTIVE step in the `po` workflow now appear as chips, ordered by `step_order`; empty-state message now points users to Users & Roles → Approval Chain Management instead of "add a role"), `src/app/(dashboard)/master-data/admin/approval-settings/page.tsx` and `src/app/(dashboard)/purchase/approval-settings/page.tsx` (removed the tabs UI — the Role Assignments tab was a stub left over from the unified-roles migration; both pages now render `<ApprovalChainsTab />` directly), deleted `src/components/purchase/ApprovalRoleAssignmentsTab.tsx`. Adding/removing a role from PO Approvals in Users & Roles is now the single source of truth for which roles can be required in an approval-chain tier.

- [2026-06-20] **Approval Settings — Live wiring to Users & Roles** — `src/components/purchase/ApprovalChainsTab.tsx` (replaced hardcoded `purchase_manager`/`accountant`/`owner` list with a live query against `custom_roles` filtered by `is_approval_slot && !deleted_at`; chips render in each role's saved color; uncovered roles render amber with a warning icon both in saved tier rows and in the New Tier / edit chip pickers; modernized New Tier card with gradient surface, labeled icon fields, live "N selected" counter, animated chip toggle), `src/hooks/useRoles.ts` (new `useApprovalRoleCoverage()` hook — queries `user_custom_roles` joined to `custom_roles` and returns a `Set<string>` of approval-slot role names that have at least one assignee; replaces the always-true stub; cache invalidation added to `useAssignRole`/`useRemoveRole`/`useUpdateRole`/`useDeleteRole` so the warning clears the instant a user is assigned in Users & Roles), `src/lib/queryKeys.ts` (added `roles.approvalCoverage` key). The old `ROLE_LABELS` lookup was already silently broken for live data (tiers store display names, not slugs), so this fixes the broken badge labels at the same time.

- [2026-06-20] **`system.admin` permission grants full-access bypass** — `src/lib/permissions.ts` (renamed `system.admin` label to "Full System Access (Owner)" with clearer description), `src/hooks/usePermissions.ts` (sets `isSystemAdmin = true` if user holds `system.admin` OR if any role has `is_system = true`), `src/lib/auth/require-admin.ts` (same dual check in `requirePermission`), `src/app/(dashboard)/admin/layout.tsx` (Contact-Centre admin pages also recognise `system.admin`), `supabase/migrations/20260620143410_seed_system_admin_on_owner_roles.sql` (seeds the new permission key on every role that currently has `is_system = true`, so Owner/Admin/Developer keep working immediately). Owners can now be authored entirely from the role-edit UI by toggling one checkbox instead of needing DB-level `is_system` flipping. Both mechanisms remain in place — backwards-compatible.

- [2026-06-20] **All nav dropdowns gated by `*.access` keys** — `src/lib/permissions.ts` (every module restructured into sub-sections per nav item, plus new top-level access keys: `master_data.access`, `purchase_sales.access`, `orders.access`, `invoices.access`, `contracts.access`, `teams.access`, `reports.access`; the standalone Sales / Quotations / Calendar modules merged into Purchase & Sales / Orders / Teams respectively), `src/components/layout/nav-config.ts` (each top-level dropdown's `permission` field set to the matching `*.access` key), `src/components/layout/NavDropdown.tsx` (strict gate — top-level permission AND inner items both required, matching MobileNavDrawer), `supabase/migrations/20260620142808_module_access_gates.sql` (backfills `*.access` for every role holding any related permission, including cross-module rules like Master Data dropdown also showing for roles holding `warehouse.access` or `teams.view`). Role-edit dialog and Permissions tab already supported sub-sections from the warehouse restructure, so no rendering changes needed.

- [2026-06-20] **Warehouse permissions restructure** — `src/lib/permissions.ts` (added `PermissionSection` type + `sections?` on `PermissionGroup`, plus `groupKeys`/`groupEntries` helpers; Warehouse module split into a top-level `warehouse.access` gate and 8 sub-sections: Warehouses / Stock Overview / Transfers / Adjustments / Inv. Checks / Stock Value / Movements / Receivals & Deliveries), `src/components/master-data/RoleFormDialog.tsx` (renders sub-section headers with their own select-all + count), `src/app/(dashboard)/master-data/users/page.tsx` (Permissions tab + RoleCard coverage chips walk both flat and sectioned permissions), `src/components/layout/nav-config.ts` + `src/lib/route-permissions.ts` (Warehouses link & route now gated on `warehouse.access`), `src/app/(dashboard)/purchase/warehouses/page.tsx` (each tab gated on its dedicated `*.view` key), `supabase/migrations/20260620141523_warehouse_perms_restructure.sql` (backfills `warehouse.access` + per-tab `*.view` keys on every existing role that already holds a matching action perm — Owner / field_rp / Inventory Manager / etc.). Old action keys (`warehouse.transfer.create`, `warehouse.adjustment.request`, etc.) untouched.

- [2026-06-20] **Demote non-seeded roles from `is_system = true`** — `supabase/migrations/20260620135552_demote_non_seeded_system_roles.sql` — A custom role (`field_rp`) had `is_system = true` set directly via Supabase Studio. Since `is_system = true` is treated as "system admin, bypass all permission checks" in client UI (`usePermissions.ts:41`), server routes (`require-admin.ts:119`), and dozens of RLS policies, any user assigned that role silently gained full access regardless of the role's `permissions` array. Migration sets `is_system = false` on every custom_role whose name isn't 'Admin' or 'Owner' — those two are the only roles intended to carry the bypass.

- [2026-06-20] **Page-level permission enforcement** — `src/lib/route-permissions.ts` (new central map: pathname-prefix → permission key, longest-prefix-wins lookup), `src/components/auth/RoutePermissionGuard.tsx` (new client guard: reads `usePathname` + `usePermissions`, renders "Access Denied" panel for unauthorized routes, passes through for system admins), `src/app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx` (wraps `{children}` in `<RoutePermissionGuard>`), `src/components/master-data/AdminSidebar.tsx` (every item now has a `permission` key; sidebar hides items the user lacks permission for and drops empty sections). Root cause: only `master-data/users/page.tsx` had a `<PermissionGate>` — every other page in `(dashboard)/` rendered for any authenticated user via direct URL navigation, regardless of role permissions. The TopNav already filtered correctly via `nav-config.ts`, so the leak was direct URL access + the unfiltered Admin Settings sidebar. Structural fix avoids editing 66 pages individually.

- [2026-06-18] **Staging DB + migrations squash** — `supabase/migrations/20240101000000_baseline_schema.sql` (new, 18,639 lines: 144 tables / 246 RLS policies / 123 functions / 47 triggers / 162 indexes / 270 FKs / 52 enums / 10 sequences captured via `pg_dump --schema-only --no-owner --no-acl`), `supabase/migrations_archive/` (345 previous migrations archived for history). Several archived migrations referenced objects created via manual SQL on prod (e.g. `call_records` table never created by any migration; `warehouse_stock_view` recreated against `warehouse_stock_allocations` 3 days before the table existed) — so a fresh `supabase db push` was impossible. Squashing into a baseline + archiving the history eliminates the problem permanently. Verified end-to-end: wipe staging public schema + `supabase db push` → 144/246/123, "Remote database is up to date." Prod migration metadata also cleaned: baseline marked applied via `migration repair --status applied`, the 345 phantom rows marked `reverted` (metadata only — prod schema/data untouched). Both prod and staging now have a single migration row (`20240101000000`), guaranteeing identical behavior on any future fresh project.

- [2026-06-14] **Wati Chat Display Fix — agent messages sent from Wati dashboard missing / mis-sided** — `src/app/api/wati/fetch-messages/route.ts`, `src/app/api/wati/webhook/route.ts`, `src/lib/contact-center/local/sync-worker.ts`, `src/lib/contact-center/local/repos/messages.ts`, `src/hooks/contact-center/local/useLocalMessages.ts`, `src/lib/contact-center/local/__tests__/sync-worker.test.ts`. Root cause was a three-layer break: (1) fetch-messages skipped every non-broadcast new agent row (`if (isNew && !isBroadcast) continue`), silently dropping replies authored in the Wati dashboard — now they flow into the upsert and existing MMS rows are still protected by the `!isNew` branch. (2) The direction classifier was strict-equal `owner === true` plus a tiny eventType list — broadened to accept the string `'true'` and Wati's actual event names (`sessionMessageSent`, `templateMessageSent`, `newSessionMessage`); a text+time customer dedup catches phantom duplicates if Wati ships an unknown shape. (3) The sync worker that feeds Dexie (which the V2 sidebar reads from) was filtered to `from_type=eq.customer` INSERTs only — so new agent rows and from_type heal UPDATEs never reached the local cache. Broadened to `event:'*'` (still one subscription, far below the original six). Plus `lazyFetch` now accepts a `force` option and `useLocalMessages` force-refreshes the active conversation on switch, so existing broken Dexie rows heal on chat open. Verified live with a fresh chat showing the 👍, two broadcast templates, and customer message all on the correct sides.

- [2026-05-12] **Contact Centre: Real-time messages + session guard + reaction architecture** — `src/app/api/wati/fetch-messages/route.ts`, `src/app/api/wati/webhook/route.ts`, `src/hooks/contact-center/useChatMessages.ts`, `src/hooks/contact-center/useLiveThread.ts`, `supabase/migrations/20260512210000_chat_messages_replica_identity.sql`, `supabase/migrations/20260512220000_cleanup_duplicate_messages.sql` — (1) useLiveThread: always syncs Wati on conversation open, adds 10s background Wati API sync, reduces poll to 2s, syncs on tab-visible/network-online events; (2) useChatMessages: session refresh guard in sendSessionMessage + sendTemplate prevents 401 when JWT expires mid-session; (3) fetch-messages: prefers wamid as external_id, pre-claims wati_-prefixed rows before upsert to eliminate duplicate agent messages, handles both separate reaction items and embedded reactions; confirmed Wati getMessages API returns 0 reaction items — customer reactions are push-only via webhook (works on Vercel, not localhost); (4) REPLICA IDENTITY FULL migration so Realtime filtered subscriptions receive UPDATE events; (5) cleanup migration removes legacy bare-id duplicates

- [2026-05-12] **Contact Centre: Webhook externalId + timestamp — fix customer reactions and message order** — `src/app/api/wati/webhook/route.ts` — (1) externalId now prefers whatsappMessageId over body.id so stored IDs match the wamid format that reaction webhooks reference; customer reactions were silently dropped because the lookup always missed; (2) timestamp now prefers body.timestamp (actual WhatsApp delivery epoch) over body.created; Wati sets body.created on bot auto-replies to the customer trigger time, causing them to sort before earlier agent messages; (3) added console.log to reaction handler for Vercel log debugging

- [2026-05-12] **Contact Centre: Instant message sync — visibility/online/realtime-error poll triggers** — `src/hooks/contact-center/useLiveThread.ts` — Poll reduced to 5s; immediate poll fires on tab-focus (visibilitychange), network-online, and Realtime CHANNEL_ERROR/TIMED_OUT; extracted applyPoll helper merges missed messages + reactions + delivery_status; convIdRef keeps event listeners in sync without re-registering

- [2026-05-12] **Contact Centre: Remove dead send_reaction Wati call, MMS-only reaction indicator** — `src/hooks/contact-center/useChatMessages.ts`, `src/components/contact-center/ChatSection.tsx`, `supabase/functions/api-wati/index.ts` — Wati has no reaction-sending API; removed send_reaction action from edge function and stripped the call from reactToMessage; ReactionBubbles now shows dashed border + "MMS" label for agent reactions (not on WhatsApp) vs solid border for customer reactions (confirmed on WhatsApp)

- [2026-05-12] **Contact Centre: RLS fix for reactions, orders 400, reactToMessage error guard** — `supabase/migrations/20260512200000_chat_messages_rls_reactions.sql`, `src/components/contact-center/OrderHistorySection.tsx`, `src/hooks/contact-center/useChatMessages.ts` — (1) Added RLS policies on chat_messages (SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE for authenticated) so browser-client reaction UPDATEs are no longer silently blocked; (2) Fixed orders query 400 by replacing non-existent services_summary column with order_type + total_amount; (3) reactToMessage now checks update error and bails early to avoid ghost reactions in local state

- [2026-05-12] **Contact Centre: Poll full-merge — reactions + delivery_status + missed messages** — `src/hooks/contact-center/useLiveThread.ts` — Added pollFromDb (last 24h window, always captures newest regardless of conversation volume); 10s poll now updates reactions and delivery_status on existing rows in addition to appending missed messages; normalized reactions null→[] everywhere

- [2026-05-12] **Contact Centre: Reaction payload fix, Realtime missed-INSERT fallback, new-message badge** — `src/hooks/contact-center/useLiveThread.ts`, `src/app/api/wati/webhook/route.ts`, `src/components/contact-center/ChatSection.tsx` — Webhook now handles both Wati reaction payload shapes (body.reaction.messageId + body.reactionMessage.key.id); Realtime UPDATE handler appends message to state if the INSERT was missed (fixes 'need Load older to see new message'); orange new-message badge appears when scrolled up and clears on scroll-to-bottom

- [2026-05-12] **Contact Centre: Cleanup duplicate rows, fix reaction lookup, send reactions via Wati** — `supabase/migrations/20260512180000_cleanup_duplicate_agent_messages.sql`, `src/app/api/wati/webhook/route.ts`, `supabase/functions/api-wati/index.ts`, `src/hooks/contact-center/useChatMessages.ts`, `src/components/contact-center/ContactCenterSidebar.tsx` — DB migration deletes old non-prefixed duplicate agent rows where wati_-prefixed twin exists; customer reaction webhook now uses .in() for prefix-aware lookup; reactToMessage calls api-wati send_reaction (best-effort) so agent emoji reactions are sent to customer on WhatsApp; empty emoji removes reaction per WhatsApp spec

- [2026-05-12] **Contact Centre: Portal emoji picker, race-condition dedup, template webhook text** — `src/components/contact-center/ChatSection.tsx`, `src/app/api/wati/webhook/route.ts` — (1) EmojiPicker replaced with React Portal at z-9999 on document.body, bypassing all stacking contexts; mouse-leave close delayed 120ms with cancel-on-enter so cursor can travel from bubble to picker; (2) Webhook race-condition fix: before inserting an outgoing message, look for a 'sending' row with same text in same conversation (last 60s) and claim it — eliminates duplicate rows and stuck spinners caused by Wati firing the webhook before the app can write the external_id; (3) extractWebhookText now falls back to template name for templates with no parseable body text

- [2026-05-12] **Contact Centre: Fix delivery spinner, template body, reaction z-index, dup-check prefix, header media** — `src/hooks/contact-center/useChatMessages.ts`, `src/app/api/wati/webhook/route.ts`, `src/components/contact-center/ChatSection.tsx`, `src/components/contact-center/ChatInputBar.tsx`, `src/types/contact-center.ts` — (1) watiId now checks message.whatsappMessageId/info.whatsAppMessageId so delivery_status advances from 'sending'; (2) sendTemplate uses named-param replacement and bodyOriginal‖elementName fallback so text is never blank; (3) loadTemplates detects HEADER DOCUMENT/IMAGE/VIDEO and adds headerMedia field; ChatInputBar shows a URL input and passes it as header parameter; (4) webhook dup-check and delivery-status update both use .in() with wati_ prefix variants to avoid duplicate rows and missed status updates; (5) EmojiPicker z-[200] + wrapper z-[100] when active so picker clears CRM column stacking context

- [2026-05-12] **Contact Centre: Fix fetch-messages 500 — restore full unique index** — `supabase/migrations/20260512170000_chat_messages_external_id_full_index.sql` — partial index (WHERE external_id IS NOT NULL) broke ON CONFLICT (external_id) in the upsert; PostgREST generates no predicate so PostgreSQL can't find the index; restored to full unique index (PostgreSQL allows multiple NULLs natively)

- [2026-05-05] **Stock Transfer Bug Fix** — `supabase/migrations/20260505000013_warehouse_manager_profile.sql`, `src/hooks/useWarehouses.ts`, `src/hooks/useWarehouseOperations.ts`, `src/components/purchase/wh/WhTransferDialog.tsx`, `src/components/purchase/wh/WhTransfersTab.tsx`, `src/components/master-data/WarehouseFormDialog.tsx` — Fixed 5 bugs: (1) canApprove always-false: warehouses.manager_id is employees FK, compared against profiles.id — added manager_profile_id (profiles FK) and fixed comparison; (2) notification banner was a lie — now inserts real in-app notification to destination manager on create, and back-notification to requester on approve/reject; (3) useWarehouseStock enabled guard: `undefined !== null` was true causing unfiltered all-stock fetch — changed to !!warehouseId; (4) useApproveTransfer missing warehouse_stock + warehouses cache invalidation; (5) WarehouseFormDialog: added System Manager (Approvals) profile dropdown

- [2026-05-05] **Warehouses Hotfix: fix UUID/TEXT cast error on reference_id** — `supabase/migrations/20260505000012_fix_reference_id_cast.sql` — Migrations 010 and 011 re-introduced `p_brand_variant_id::TEXT` in the `inventory_stock_movements` INSERT; since `reference_id` is UUID, this caused "column reference_id is of type uuid but expression is of type text" when adding/editing brand variants; migration 012 removes the `::TEXT` cast in both the increase and decrease movement log paths
- [2026-05-05] **Warehouses Hotfix: surface allocation errors + fresh cache** — `src/components/services/inventory/BrandVariantEditDialog.tsx`, `src/hooks/useInventory.ts`, `supabase/migrations/20260505000011_allocate_warehouse_stock_verify.sql` — applyAllocations re-throws RPC errors so success toast is blocked if allocation fails; useVariantWarehouseStock staleTime reduced to 0 so dialog always loads live DB state on reopen; migration 011 adds end-of-function verification that FIFO final qty equals target, raising exception on mismatch
- [2026-05-05] **Warehouses Hotfix: unassigned stock + stale zero-cost layers** — `supabase/migrations/20260505000010_fix_allocate_cost_on_all_delta_paths.sql` — Fixed two bugs in `allocate_warehouse_stock`: (1) after any qty increase/decrease, now also updates `unit_cost`/`total_unit_cost` on all existing opening-stock layers (`receival_id IS NULL`) for that warehouse, correcting historical zero-cost layers; (2) confirms gap-fill correctly reads DB stock_level set by the variant UPDATE before RPC fires; also fixed frontend `applyAllocations` to include all warehouses with existing stock so cost-only saves propagate
- [2026-05-05] **Warehouses Hotfix: avg_cost cost-only update** — `supabase/migrations/20260505000009_fix_allocate_warehouse_stock_cost_update.sql` — Fixed `allocate_warehouse_stock` RPC: when `v_delta = 0` and a positive unit cost is provided, now updates `unit_cost`/`total_unit_cost` on existing opening-stock FIFO layers (`receival_id IS NULL`) for the warehouse, then calls `recalc_average_cost`; previously returned early so cost edits never propagated to `warehouse_stock_view`

Top "+Free" button opens Category→Item→Brand picker for non-PO free goods. Free items use `is_free:true, unit_cost:0` and skip `received_qty` update. — `src/components/purchase/PoReceiveTab.tsx`
- [2026-04-24] **Dropdown UUID Guard rule** — Added mandatory checklist to `AGENTS.md` requiring all dropdowns to display human-readable labels, never raw UUIDs.
- [2026-04-24] **Receival rejection rollback** — `useApproveReceival` now decrements `po_line_items.received_qty` for each non-free item when a receival is rejected, so the Receive tab correctly reflects remaining quantities. — `src/hooks/useReceivals.ts`
- [2026-04-24] **useInventoryCategories 400 fix** — Removed `.eq('status', 'active')` filter; `inventory_categories` has no `status` column. Categories now load correctly and the "+ Free" dialog in PoReceiveTab works. — `src/hooks/useInventory.ts`
- [2026-04-24] **PO Activity Log** — New `poActivityLogger.ts` utility writes to `activity_log` for every PO lifecycle event: PO Created, Draft Saved, Submitted for Approval, PO Amended (version), PO Cancelled, Approval step approved/force-approved/rejected, PO Fully Approved, Receival Recorded/Approved/Rejected, Payment Recorded. Activity tab in PoDetailDialog shows color-coded timeline dots and severity badges. — `src/lib/poActivityLogger.ts`, `src/hooks/usePOApprovals.ts`, `src/hooks/usePurchaseOrders.ts`, `src/hooks/useReceivals.ts`, `src/components/purchase/PoDetailDialog.tsx`
- [2026-04-24] **Submit for Approval fix** — `PoDetailDialog` "Submit for Approval" button was calling `useSubmitPO` (status-change only) instead of `useSubmitPOForApproval` (creates approval steps + notifications). New POs now correctly generate `po_approvals` rows and notify all approvers. — `src/components/purchase/PoDetailDialog.tsx`
- [2026-04-24] **Multi-select status filter + Cancel PO** — Status filter on PO list is now a multi-select DropdownMenu with color-coded checkboxes; filtering moved client-side. "Delete PO" row action replaced with "Cancel PO" (sets `status=cancelled`, keeps record visible in list; hidden for already-cancelled POs). — `src/app/(dashboard)/purchase/orders/page.tsx`
